# Composability Index — Wiki Unlocks Registry

**Summary**: Scannable registry of every known and candidate **unlock** in the wiki — pairs (or triples) of frameworks, techniques, or substrates that produce a *qualitatively new capability* when combined that neither has alone. The page exists so the question *"what can I unlock by combining X with Y?"* has a single discoverable answer rather than requiring full-wiki re-derivation each time.

**Sources**:
- Synthesised from wiki cross-references and conversation history (2026-05-11)
- Architectural primitive defined in [substrate-algorithm-composition](./substrate-algorithm-composition.md)
- All entries trace to existing owner pages — this page is an index, not a source

## How to read this page

Each row in the tables below names a **composition**:
- **Components** — what gets combined (always 2 or 3 wiki concepts)
- **Pattern** — the architectural primitive (currently always [substrate-algorithm-composition](./substrate-algorithm-composition.md), but more patterns will accrue)
- **Unlock** — the new capability that emerges; what *neither* component has alone
- **Failure mode** — what breaks if the composition is botched
- **Status** — `confirmed` (instance exists, mechanism understood), `candidate` (plausible but not built/tested), `aspirational` (worth attempting)

Use this page in three ways:

1. **Read top-to-bottom** to see every known unlock at a glance.
2. **Scan the Components column** for a concept you care about (e.g. *Soroban*) to find every confirmed and candidate composition involving it.
3. **Look at the Candidate Unlocks section** for compositions worth trying — these are the highest-leverage experimental directions.

**Registry vs. ledger**: this page is the *registry* — structured rows with components, pattern, unlock, failure mode, and owner. The narrated *witness* of Knowing-tier ([SPARK](./spark-overview.md) T3) events lives in wiki/unlocks/ as append-only ceremony entries. Confirmed rows here typically have a matching ledger entry there once SPARK fires; candidate rows do not until promoted.

| Soroban place-value columns + A–Z acrostic food-peg list (column identity) | [substrate-algorithm-composition](./substrate-algorithm-composition.md) | Each place value gets a vivid food identity (`A`=ones, `B`=tens, `C`=hundreds, …) so a multi-digit number reads as a **string of foods**: `1598` = Donut·Croissant·Banana·Apple carrying `1·5·9·8`. The peg names *which place*; the beads still hold the digit — column-identity anchors that stop the places blurring during mental soroban. **Flipping** a peg upside-down extends the same set *below* the ones (flipped Apple = tenths, flipped Banana = hundredths), so decimals reuse the food set with no new pegs and the unit never re-anchors. The slot-to-slot mapping for this composition:

| BRIDGE LOAD output | NEDF slot | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| Source-domain image (Retrieve) | Name-hook | Perceptual entry — independent of the analogy's structural work |

| Nearest-neighbor / forbidden transfer (Guard) | Distinguisher | Blocks the most likely confusion |
| Boundary / breakpoints (Guard) | Failure | Where the analogy stops working *becomes* where the concept fails |

The Name-hook is the one NEDF slot BRIDGE doesn't fill — perceptual access is upstream of analogy, not downstream. This is why folding BRIDGE *into* NEDF would be wrong: BRIDGE feeds 3 of 4 slots, but not the entry trigger, and BRIDGE also feeds SPEAR and CAST elsewhere.

| hacking-art-of-exploitation-erickson (silicon mechanism) × rtfm-clark-downer (command catalog) × stuttard-pinto-web-app-hackers-handbook (web altitude) — instance 1 trio · Erickson × RTFM × mcnab-network-security-assessment (protocol-decision-tree) — instance 2 · Erickson × RTFM × oconnor-violent-python (script-glue) — instance 3 · Erickson × kennedy-metasploit-pentesters-guide (framework-orchestration) × RTFM — instance 4 · Erickson × RTFM × sikorski-honig-practical-malware-analysis (reverse-symmetry) — instance 5 · RTFM × seitz-gray-hat-python (tool-construction) — instance 6 (binary degenerate) × [substrate-algorithm-composition](./substrate-algorithm-composition.md) (parent pattern) | **N-ary substrate composition** (generalizes substrate × operational pair to substrate × operational × X with open arity, N=6 instances) | **The substrate × operational composition pattern is N-ary, not just binary.** M11.1 registered the binary substrate × operational pair (Erickson × RTFM as silicon × command) at N=2. ### 2026-06-09 GoF Design Patterns ingest — 2 confirmed unlocks at the software-architecture ↔ wiki-governance layer

| Components | Pattern | Unlock | Failure mode | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| GoF "**Program to an interface, not an implementation**" (design-pattern-intuition, source: GOF-articulo.pdf p.18) × CLAUDE.md §Consistency `no-parallel-definitions` rule × [glossary](./glossary.md) (interface registry) × the `wiki-link` mechanism | **convergence (independent re-derivation)** — empirical wiki rule = 1994 OO principle | **The wiki's anti-drift governance rule *is* "program to an interface" applied to prose.** A non-owner page must never redefine a registered term — it links to the owner instead. That is exactly GoF's principle: a page depends on the glossary's **interface** (term name + owner pointer), never on another page's **implementation** (its full definition). GoF even define **abstract coupling** ("class A holds a reference to abstract class B"), which is precisely what an owner-page `wiki-link` is. The convergence (the wiki built this empirically; GoF derived it for software in 1994) is evidence the rule is *real*, not arbitrary, and it hands the wiki a precise refactoring vocabulary: a parallel definition is "implementation coupling between pages"; the fix is "abstract-couple via the glossary." | Over-literalizing — citing the principle to justify *under*-linking until pages can't be read without round-trips; or treating every cross-page reference as needing a Facade/Adapter when a plain `wiki-link` suffices. | design-pattern-intuition, [software-design-principles-for-neural-os](./software-design-principles-for-neural-os.md), [glossary](./glossary.md) |
| GoF **Table 1.2** ("what each pattern lets you vary independently", source: GOF-articulo.pdf p.30) × [substrate-algorithm-composition](./substrate-algorithm-composition.md) (parent pattern) × [composability-index](./composability-index.md) (this registry) | **substrate-algorithm-composition** (cross-altitude structural match) | **"Encapsulate what varies" is substrate-algorithm-composition one altitude down.** GoF Table 1.2 is a 23-row registry: each pattern isolates *one* design aspect behind a stable interface so it can vary without redesign. This registry is the same shape one altitude up — isolate the changing aspect so it composes into new capability. The unlock gives the composability-index a 30-year-proven software ancestor **and a generative probe**: GoF's own pattern-selection question — *"consider what should be variable in your design"* — becomes a method for finding new wiki unlocks (name the change-point, find the row whose "what varies" column matches). | Conflating GoF "varies independently" (one axis isolated per pattern) with the wiki's "composes into an emergent third capability" (two substrates → new behavior) — they rhyme but aren't identical; jamming all 23 patterns into this registry as rows would dilute it. | [substrate-algorithm-composition](./substrate-algorithm-composition.md), design-pattern-intuition, gof-design-patterns |

Plus 1 **candidate** (flag-only): GoF's 13-section pattern-description template (Intent / Applicability / Consequences / Related Patterns) × the wiki page format + [NEDF](./nedf-overview.md) — Applicability = NEDF Distinguisher + drill-ladder trigger phrase; Consequences = the wiki's "failure mode" column; Related Patterns = `wiki-links`. ### 2026-06-09 The Pragmatic Programmer ingest — 2 confirmed unlocks + DRY re-parents Unlock ①

**Re-parenting note.** DRY (PP Tip 11) is the *general parent* of the GoF "program to an interface" unlock (① above): program-to-interface is **DRY for behavioral contracts**, and the glossary's `no-parallel-definitions` rule is **DRY for definitions**. The GoF entry above stands as written; this ingest reframes it as one of two special cases of a single, more fundamental law — the cleaner topology is one root principle (DRY) with two instances.

| Components | Pattern | Unlock | Failure mode | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DRY (PP Tip 11 — "single, unambiguous, authoritative representation within a system", source: the-pragmatic-programmer.pdf p.27) × CLAUDE.md `no-parallel-definitions` × [glossary](./glossary.md) (interface registry) × PP Tip 54 "Use a Project Glossary" | **convergence (parent law)** — DRY governs both the GoF behavioral unlock and the wiki's definition rule | **DRY applied to a knowledge base *is* the wiki's anti-drift governance — and PP independently names the exact mechanism the wiki built.** The book recommends *both* DRY (Tip 11) *and* a single project glossary (Tip 54, "a single source of all the specific terms and vocabulary for a project"); the wiki has both, and they are the same mechanism. This generalizes Unlock ①: program-to-interface is DRY for behavior; `no-parallel-definitions` is DRY for definitions. The number-drift linter (`.claude/wiki-precommit-lint.py`) is, structurally, a DRY-violation detector. The 1999 software book and the 2026 LLM-wiki re-derived one law in different substrates. | Over-literalizing DRY to justify *under*-linking until pages can't be read without round-trips; a wiki-link is a reference, not a duplicated copy of knowledge. | dry-principle, [glossary](./glossary.md), design-pattern-intuition |
| Knowledge Portfolio (PP Tip 8 + its five investment rules, source: the-pragmatic-programmer.pdf p.13) × Neural OS Research (this repo's thesis) × [METER](./meter-overview.md) review cadence | **convergence (founding metaphor)** — the repo *is* a managed knowledge portfolio | **The repo's founding metaphor has a named 1999 source, and the five investment rules become operating rules.** Invest regularly = the METER weekly cadence + the `/ingest` habit; diversify = the palace's spread across domains; manage risk = deep tactical drills balanced against speculative meta-knowledge pages; buy low = ingesting an unfamiliar source before its relevance is obvious; review & rebalance = spaced-repetition review + re-tagging stale pages. | A portfolio never rebalanced rots — skills decay into broken windows; without "invest regularly as a habit," ingest goes sporadic and the palace stales. | knowledge-portfolio, [meter-overview](./meter-overview.md), pragmatic-programmer |

Plus 2 **candidates** (flag-only): (a) Orthogonality (PP Tip 13, "changes in one do not affect any of the others") × the four-axis `palace × level × domain × para` addressing (para-method, full-tracking-system-roadmap) — the claim those four axes are independent *is* an orthogonality decision, and PP supplies the test the wiki should run on itself (`cross_axis_ripple_count`, target 0). (b) **Broken Windows** (PP Tip 4) × `.claude/wiki-precommit-lint.py` — the linter is broken-window enforcement: fix the smell on sight before entropy normalizes the next one. See pragmatic-programmer.

| Components | Pattern | Unlock | Failure mode | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| loop-invariants (CLRS §2.1 — Initialization: true before first iteration; Maintenance: if true before iteration, true after; Termination: provides useful property at loop end; source: introduction-to-algorithms-clrs.pdf Ch.2) × design by contract (owned by pragmatic-programmer — precondition / invariant / post-condition; source: the-pragmatic-programmer.pdf p.109, Tip 31) | **convergence (independent re-derivation)** — two sources 10 years apart re-derived the same correctness contract | **Loop invariants and Design by Contract are each other's missing half.** CLRS's Init/Maintenance/Termination triad *is* the algorithmic-register instantiation of DbC's pre/invariant/post-condition contract: Init = precondition holds; Maintenance = invariant is preserved through each step; Termination = postcondition holds. A loop invariant *is* a design-by-contract assertion on the loop's logical state. The missing halves: DbC gives runtime-assertion enforcement (the assertions fire); the loop invariant gives the *proof schema* (here is why the assertion must hold at each boundary). Together they form a unified algorithmic-contract notation — the loop invariant tells you *what to assert*; DbC tells you *where and how to enforce it*. Convergence evidence: both CLRS (1989, orig. edition; 2009, 3rd ed.) and Hunt-Thomas (1999) independently codified the same three-checkpoint contract without citing each other. | Students treat loop invariants as "exam ritual" (write the magic phrase, move on) and DbC as "docstring convention" (add the annotation, forget it) — same logic in both cases, never named once, so neither becomes reflexive. The cost is that correctness reasoning stays ceremonial rather than becoming a reflex that fires during code review and debugging. **Trophy locus: peg #74** ([peg-audio-visual-matrix](./peg-audio-visual-matrix.md)) — carried here 2026-08-14 from the `wiki/unlocks/` T3 stub, which was retired that day; this row is now the unlock's only home. | loop-invariants, design by contract, pragmatic-programmer |
| graph-theory-overview (Axenovich KIT 2020 — mathematical properties: connectivity κ/λ, DAG structure / topological ordering, graph distance and BFS-tree structure; source: ingested 2026-05-27) × CLRS Ch.22 bfs / dfs / topological-sort (graph algorithm behavior; source: introduction-to-algorithms-clrs.pdf Part VI) | **substrate-algorithm-composition** — mathematical properties of the graph explain the algorithm's guarantees | **Explain *why* BFS yields shortest paths and DFS finish-times yield topological order from the graph's own structure, not as memorized facts.** BFS yields shortest unweighted paths because the graph's distance metric (minimum edge-hops) is exactly what BFS's queue discipline optimizes — each wavefront expands at uniform cost, and graph theory's definition of distance is min-edge-path. DFS finish-times yield topological order in a DAG because a DAG's acyclicity (no back-edges under DFS) means the last vertex to finish must come first in topological order — this is Axenovich's DAG characterization (a DAG has at least one vertex of in-degree 0, recurse) expressed as a DFS invariant. Without the mathematical substrate, both facts are magic mnemonics; with it, they are theorems you can re-derive from the graph's properties. | Graph theory and graph algorithms stay siloed: a learner can classify a graph (this is a DAG, this is bipartite) but cannot derive the algorithm's behavior from those properties — they memorize "BFS = shortest paths" without understanding why, and the algorithm becomes a black box that fails under novel conditions. | graph-theory-overview, bfs, dfs, topological-sort, introduction-to-algorithms |

Plus 2 **candidates** (flag-only):

(a) **asymptotic-notation ⊕ "good-enough software"** — asymptotic notation gives a formal cost-reasoning backbone ("is O(n log n) acceptable for n≤10⁶?") to the Pragmatic Programmer's informal good-enough judgment (Tip 7: "Know When to Stop"). The formal vocabulary (Θ-class, constant factors, input-size regimes) operationalizes what Hunt-Thomas leave as engineering taste. Candidate because the composition is framing-level — it sharpens judgment, but no new mechanism is built; the built artifact would be a decision checklist that maps O-class × input-size to good-enough verdict.

(b) **master-theorem as recursion's complexity oracle** — given any recursive algorithm whose structure fits the form T(n) = aT(n/b) + f(n), the master theorem yields the Θ-class in closed form without solving the recurrence. This within-domain grounding is now wired (the recursion and merge-sort pages link to master-theorem); the unlock becomes confirmed when a drill ladder stage covers "identify a, b, f(n) from code → apply the correct case → state the Θ-class" as a <5s reflex. See master-theorem.

**Context.** Six domain-application pages ([memory-atomic-design](./memory-atomic-design.md) · [money-atomic-design](./money-atomic-design.md) · [problem-solving-atomic-design](./problem-solving-atomic-design.md) · [logic-atomic-design](./logic-atomic-design.md) · [visualization-atomic-design](./visualization-atomic-design.md) · sound-relationship-house as the 6th relational instance) had independently applied a 5-tier Atoms→Molecules→Organisms→Templates→Pages spine to their respective domains — the first five before the canonical Frost source was ingested. The canonical owner atomic-design now sits as the parent all six specialize. This is structurally identical to the GoF convergence above: a source the wiki had been citing by its effects for months without an owner page.

| Components | Pattern | Unlock | Failure mode | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| atomic-design (Brad Frost 2013/2016 — 5-tier methodology: Atoms · Molecules · Organisms · Templates · Pages; chemistry-origin mental model; "not linear / not rigid dogma"; 3 advantages; applies to all UIs) × [memory-atomic-design](./memory-atomic-design.md) · [money-atomic-design](./money-atomic-design.md) · [problem-solving-atomic-design](./problem-solving-atomic-design.md) · [logic-atomic-design](./logic-atomic-design.md) · [visualization-atomic-design](./visualization-atomic-design.md) · sound-relationship-house (6 domain-application sisters) | **convergence (independent re-derivation at scale)** — the wiki re-derived Frost's 5-tier spine across 6 independent domains before ingesting the canonical source | **Independent 6-domain re-derivation is strong evidence the lens is real and domain-portable.** The six sister pages each applied a 5-tier compositional hierarchy without citing a shared ancestor: Memory (37 atoms · 17 molecules · 12 organisms · 13 templates), Money (40 atoms · 20 molecules · 12 organisms · 13 templates), Problem-Solving (32 atoms · 6 molecules · organisms · templates), Logic (~80 atoms · named fallacies · deduction system · argument grids), Visualization (scene-grammar elements → composition principles → frameworks → full pipelines), and Sound-Relationship-House (Gottman's 7 floors as the 6th relational instance). The canonical atomic-design owner now provides the parent all six specialize: Frost's chemistry metaphor, the "not a linear process / mental model" framing, and the 3 advantages (hierarchy · modularity · reuse) are the exact machinery the sisters instantiate. N=6 independent instances = the highest evidence count for any single lens in the wiki; the structural twin of the recent GoF "program to an interface" convergence. The canonical owner also gives the wiki the failure-mode vocabulary it had been missing: Frost explicitly names the trap of "treating pages as templates" and "designing in isolation without a shared system" — both are now in atomic-design §Failure modes and propagate back to the six sisters as the named anti-pattern. | Treating atomic-design as a 7th sibling rather than the parent — the per-domain pages must link UP to atomic-design as owner (now done via link-weaver), never redefine the 5 tiers locally. If any sister page adds a "What is Atomic Design?" section, that is a parallel definition and a lint violation. The 5-tier ladder belongs exclusively to atomic-design; sisters own their domain instantiation, not the underlying structure. | atomic-design, [memory-atomic-design](./memory-atomic-design.md), [money-atomic-design](./money-atomic-design.md), [problem-solving-atomic-design](./problem-solving-atomic-design.md), [logic-atomic-design](./logic-atomic-design.md), [visualization-atomic-design](./visualization-atomic-design.md), sound-relationship-house |

### 2026-06-16 Domain-dialects convergence — 1 confirmed unlock at the explanation-tooling ↔ OCP layer

**Context.** Independently re-derived from the "explanation leverage" direction: the observation that no single explanation fits all domains — that the highest-leverage explanatory language needs a thin general spine plus swappable domain-specific dialects. This *is* the Open/Closed Principle the wiki already states in [software-design-principles-for-neural-os](./software-design-principles-for-neural-os.md) §O ("specialized UMTF presets for codebases, math, history, or people"), reached without citing it — the same convergence shape as the GoF "program to an interface" unlock above.

| Components | Pattern | Unlock | Failure mode | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| "No one explanation fits all domains → thin general spine + swappable domain dialects" (re-derived 2026-06-16) × [software-design-principles-for-neural-os](./software-design-principles-for-neural-os.md) §O Open/Closed + §D Dependency Inversion × [UMTF](./universal-mental-tagging-framework.md) (the spine) × [factual-knowledge-precedes-skill](./factual-knowledge-precedes-skill.md) (cognitive-science floor) × tools-over-intelligence §Primitives-not-products | **convergence (independent re-derivation)** — explanation-leverage intuition = the OCP extension-point the wiki already named | **The "domain-specific tools have most leverage" intuition *is* OCP read from the explanation direction, and it now has a falsifiable contract.** A general explainer caps out because (per [factual-knowledge-precedes-skill](./factual-knowledge-precedes-skill.md)) thinking skills don't exist independently of the domain — generality cannot exploit the domain schema that makes reasoning cheap. The resolution is a layered split: a closed general spine ([UMTF](./universal-mental-tagging-framework.md)'s 7 families + encoder spine, reused as **Flyweight**) plus open per-domain dialects (selected by **Strategy**), governed by a three-guardrail legitimacy contract (no new framework/acronym · orthogonality lock · registration requirement) and a METER reuse floor that demotes unused dialects. Leverage lives in the composition, not at either pole — the same lesson as tools-over-intelligence: stock primitives, fossilize per-domain tools on top. First application target: the `/explain` skill (one visual spine, per-domain explanation grammars via Strategy). | Dialect sprawl — minting a bespoke dialect with fresh vocabulary per domain re-opens the spine and turns cognitive-overhead + retrieval-clarity negative; the guardrails + reuse floor exist precisely to prevent this. The opposite over-correction is refusing all dialects and forcing one universal explainer, which caps sharpness in every domain. | [software-design-principles-for-neural-os](./software-design-principles-for-neural-os.md), [universal-mental-tagging-framework](./universal-mental-tagging-framework.md), [factual-knowledge-precedes-skill](./factual-knowledge-precedes-skill.md), tools-over-intelligence |

### 2026-06-16 Idle-resource-is-adversarial — 1 confirmed convergence unlock at the resource-stewardship layer

**Context.** Surfaced from a conversation about resource management (not an ingest): the claim that every resource must be deployed with intention or it is turned against you, with an inventory-first prerequisite ("know thyself"). Validated via `/validate-idea` (keep-with-modification: no acronym, bound "not winning = losing" to decaying/capturable resources, file as a lens + convergence unlock). The unlock is the recognition that three wiki domains already derived the same law independently — the signature of [cross-tradition-convergence-pattern](./cross-tradition-convergence-pattern.md).

| Components | Pattern | Unlock | Failure mode | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Cantillon effect** (monetary-system-what-is-money — idle cash is the most-taxed position) × **attack surface** (memory-segment-attack-surface, social-engineering-hadnagy — unused service/account = exploitable) × **substrate stewardship** ([logicians-madness-substrate-thesis](./logicians-madness-substrate-thesis.md) — unmaintained capacity corrodes the worker) × **the sluggard's field + the buried talent** (bible-money-stewardship-principles — "an idle asset doesn't stay neutral, it decays and is captured," Prov 24:30-34; the talent returned *intact* is condemned, Matt 25:25-27 — added 2026-07-25) × the inventory-first prerequisite ([frame-forge](./frame-forge.md) Inventory step; defensive asset-inventory) | **cross-tradition convergence (independent re-derivation, N=4 domains)** — money, cybersecurity, cognitive-substrate, and Scripture each state "idle resource is adversarial by default" with no shared lineage | **One resource-stewardship law owns three scattered instances.** Idle resource is not neutral: subject to decay or capture, it decays or is turned against you. Prerequisite = inventory (know thyself); action = intentional deployment; discriminator = **intentional vs. idle, not held vs. spent** (a reserve with a named job is deployed, not idle). New capability: a single diagnostic ("is this resource catalogued and assigned a job?") routes across money, security, attention, and skills at once; METER event `resource.inventory_review` flags `idle-without-intent`. Owner: idle-resource-is-adversarial. | Over-reading it as "save nothing" — gutting the room-for-error resilience buffer; the discriminator (intentional-vs-idle) is the guard. Opposite over-correction: treating genuinely inert resources as if they decay, manufacturing false urgency outside the scope bound. | idle-resource-is-adversarial, monetary-system-what-is-money, memory-segment-attack-surface, [logicians-madness-substrate-thesis](./logicians-madness-substrate-thesis.md), [cross-tradition-convergence-pattern](./cross-tradition-convergence-pattern.md) |

(b) Correction: the strict **quota** becomes a **quality gate** — `concept.has_visual` is subordinated to `concept.visual_shows_form`, *no visual beats a decorative one*, and the ideal visual degrades with reader expertise (Kalyuga expertise-reversal). New METER: `concept.visual_shows_form` (primary) > `concept.has_visual` (subordinate) + `concept.decoration_by_mandate` (defect, floor 0). | Reading the evidence as "fewer visuals" (it rewards *relevance*, not lower count); importing the **"visual learner" myth** as justification (debunked — Pashler 2008; aphantasics reason fine — Zeman 2015/2020); citing the fabricated Dale's-Cone "remember X%" retention numbers (Subramony 2014). | [visual-thinking-evidence](./visual-thinking-evidence.md), [feedback-visual-per-concept](./feedback-visual-per-concept.md), [show-vs-say](./show-vs-say.md) |
| Ocean/drop bandwidth split ([visual-thinking-evidence](./visual-thinking-evidence.md): perception ~10⁷–10⁹ bits/s vs cognition ~10 bits/s) × internal-automaticity tools ([memory-palace-architecture-for-neural-os](./memory-palace-architecture-for-neural-os.md) / [soroban-learning-method](./soroban-learning-method.md) / [CAST](./cast-overview.md)) × externalization (NIPSF Instrument layer / [show-vs-say](./show-vs-say.md)) | bandwidth-channel routing (where a representation is *read* decides its function) | **Principled answer to "does automaticity replace paper?"** Automaticity makes the *internal* cells cheaper, so it replaces paper for **mastered recall & execution** — but it cannot replace paper for **novel structure**, because only externalizing routes structure through high-bandwidth **perception** (the ocean), while internal replay (however automatic) stays in the serial ~10 bits/s conscious channel (the drop). Same reason aphantasia doesn't cripple reasoning. Yields the **thinking-tool vs memory-tool 2×2**: thinking = external+structural, memory = internal+vivid. | Claiming a trained palace replaces paper for *novel* reasoning (collapses the external-structural cell into the internal channel); running reasoning *inside* vivid mental imagery (bottlenecked, can impede — Knauff 2002); over-generalizing the expert-mental-calculator edge case beyond narrow over-learned domains. | [visual-thinking-evidence](./visual-thinking-evidence.md), tools-over-intelligence |

### 2026-07-19 Process-over-outcome / test-pyramid convergence `/validate-idea` — 1 confirmed unlock

**Context.** Surfaced from a strategy conversation (not an ingest): the claim that the *end result* shouldn't be the focus — the *process/automation, run now,* should be — "similar to unit tests: with perfect unit tests, end-to-end tests won't matter much; concentrating on 'now' + automation raises the probability of winning." Validated via `/validate-idea` (keep-with-modification: no acronym; file as a lens + convergence unlock on [software-design-principles-for-neural-os](./software-design-principles-for-neural-os.md); bound "e2e won't matter" to *thin, not zero*; operational home = genius-compass). Third instance of the "software-testing principle ≡ wiki-governance principle" family, after the GoF *program-to-interface* (Unlock ①) and DRY convergences above.

| Components | Pattern | Unlock | Failure mode | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Test pyramid (broad automated unit base / thin e2e apex) × [METER](./meter-overview.md) (process events = leading indicators; periodic reports = lagging) × genius-compass (weekly process-audit, not annual outcome reflection) × the falsifier / pass-floor discipline × [software-design-principles-for-neural-os](./software-design-principles-for-neural-os.md) §The Main Constraint | **convergence (independent re-derivation)** — a software-testing heuristic = the wiki's process-over-outcome governance; sibling to Unlock ① (GoF program-to-interface) and its DRY parent | **Optimize + automate the controllable process now; keep only a thin check on the outcome.** The pyramid's logic — verify each unit cheaply so the expensive whole-system check stays small — read as strategy is: measure and automate the *leading-indicator* process (the units), and the *lagging* outcome ("did I win?") mostly follows, so it needs only a thin periodic check. This is why concentrating on the process *now* raises the probability of the result more than staring at the result does. New capability: a falsifiable **ratio floor** — a roadmap's automated process-measurement events must dominate, outcome-checks stay thin-but-present; if outcome-checking exceeds process-measurement the roadmap is *outcome-anxious* and refactors toward process. genius-compass is the built weekly instance (Energy-delta + trajectory = the broad process base; Strategic Leverage + threshold-breach checks = the thin outcome apex). **Base unit = the slope, not the position:** for a person the leading indicator is *effectiveness-now* = `d(progress)/dt` sampled at ~1s, not the win (its integral `∫ effectiveness dt`, readable only at the finish) — you can steer only the present slope, never the accumulated position, so METER `effectiveness.slope_sample` produces the base and the Compass's weekly trajectory is its running integral (finest layer of the temporal substrate stack, and the only one that is a *rate* not a *level*). | **Thin, not zero.** "Perfect units → e2e irrelevant" holds *only when the units compose correctly*; the classic failure is 100% unit coverage with a broken integration seam — the analog of a flawless automated process optimizing the *wrong* leading indicator (perfect execution, no win). Drop the outcome apex entirely and you license process-theater that never checks whether the process reaches the goal — the genius-compass "flow-state video-game marked as Genius" misfire. Opposite over-correction: fattening the apex back into outcome-anxiety, the very state the principle exists to leave. In derivative form the same failure restates as: a single-instant slope read is noise (needs a window > 0), and a steep slope up the *wrong* hill is magnitude without direction — the broken-seam bug once more. | [software-design-principles-for-neural-os](./software-design-principles-for-neural-os.md), genius-compass, [meter-overview](./meter-overview.md), [composability-index](./composability-index.md) |

### 2026-07-26 First-fraction rule — 1 confirmed convergence unlock across the canon boundary

**Context.** Landing a pointer that had been dangling since the bible-money-stewardship-principles ingest: §F3 promised its full unlock to money-canon-synthesis, which contained no biblical content at all. Surfaced during the 2026-07-25 fusion pass, relabelled *pending* rather than left as a false pointer, and filled in here. Not a `/validate-idea` case — no framework, no acronym, no new page; it is a convergence recorded on an existing synthesis page.

| Components | Pattern | Unlock | Failure mode | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Firstfruits** (bible-money-stewardship-principles §F3 — the first *and the best*, before consumption) × **Clason's self-keep tenth** (richest-man-in-babylon, seven-cures-lean-purse Cure 1) × **Pay Yourself First** (rich-dad-poor-dad, term owner) × **the Profit First formula flip** (profit-first — `Sales − Profit = Expenses`) | **cross-tradition convergence (independent re-derivation, N=4)** spanning ~3 millennia with no shared lineage | **The machinery is identical; only the beneficiary differs — and one of the four is not a beneficiary at all.** All four allocate a fixed fraction *before* discretion, by **rule rather than willpower**, on the shared empirical premise that whatever remains after discretionary spending is reliably nothing. Two of them make the same sharper second move — removing the money from **view**, not just from the budget (EGW's "before any portion is consumed" ≡ Profit First's small-plate psychology): visible balance, not virtue, governs consumption. Clason/Kiyosaki/Michalowicz move money between *your own* pockets; firstfruits **alienates** it, which is the F1 ownership inversion applied to the allocation mechanism. **The gap it exposes:** the canon has *priority* (pay first) and *proportion* (a tenth; TAP), but **no analog of quality** — no book asks whether the fraction is your *best* rather than merely your *first*, a question that only becomes meaningful once the fraction leaves your hands. Shared falsifier: a first-fraction rule is only real if it **survives a bad month**. | Collapsing it into "the 10% is the same thing" — the richest-man-in-babylon collision note stands: a kept tenth and a surrendered tithe share a fraction, not a meaning. The convergence is one level down, at the machinery. Opposite over-correction: reading the shared mechanism as proof the destinations are interchangeable. Third: treating the four as agreeing on *cost* — they do not (Kiyosaki wants the cash crunch, Michalowicz ramps it away via target-allocation-percentages, room-for-error sides with Michalowicz, Scripture renders regardless). | money-canon-synthesis §Convergence 7, bible-money-stewardship-principles, richest-man-in-babylon, rich-dad-poor-dad, profit-first |

| **Perishable/storable cut** (idle-resource-is-adversarial §The perishable/storable cut — can the resource be held at all?) × esbi-quadrant (E/S vs B/I) × money-canon-synthesis §Convergence 1 ("time is the variable that cannot be bought back") × **biblical capital vs provision** (bible-money-stewardship-principles — multiply vs circulate, sourced to `Bible-Research/Research/bible-investment-as-increase.md`) | **Flyweight** (one axis reused across domain instances) + **Strategy** (domain selects its dialect: ESBI in money, capital/provision in Scripture) — the same pair the parent lens already registers | **Kiyosaki's left/right line and Scripture's capital/provision line are the same cut read with opposite mandates on money — and the cut supplies the mechanism the money canon asserts without explaining.** money-canon-synthesis §Convergence 1 states that time cannot be bought back but never says *why* time-for-money caps out; the answer is that a perishable input has no stock behind it, so it cannot compound regardless of who owns its slope — which also makes perishable-input dependence the **cause** beneath the time-coupling axis (time-coupling is what you observe; this is why). The two dialects then diverge exactly on money: the canon files money as the storable thing to accumulate into an asset column, Scripture files it under *provision* (use and circulate) and prescribes conversion into the imperishable rather than accumulation — a genuine tension now recorded as money-canon-synthesis §Tension 5 and the doctrinal rung on bible-money-stewardship-principles. New capability: one classifier routes both frames, and the perishable side gets a stricter METER floor (a perishable resource cannot be justified by "held with intention"). | Naming drift back to "cyclic" — the word already means a loop-cue in [universal-mental-tagging-framework](./universal-mental-tagging-framework.md) §Temporal, acyclic/DAG in graph theory, and the assessment loop in mcnab-network-security-assessment; three meanings in one wiki is the orthogonality failure the rename exists to prevent. Second: over-reading the biblical half as anti-saving — Scripture's own bible-money-stewardship-principles §F4 Joseph buffer is the counter-instance, and the discriminator there is two-axis prudence, not accumulate-vs-not. Third: importing the doctrinal "treasure in heaven" rung onto the systems page, which would break that page's SRP by hosting a soteriological claim in a governance lens. | idle-resource-is-adversarial, esbi-quadrant, money-canon-synthesis, bible-money-stewardship-principles, money-applied-judgment |

### 2026-07-29 The day as a conversion loop `/validate-idea` — 1 candidate unlock at the resource-stewardship layer

**Context.** Surfaced from a conversation extending the money-rooms Coda (accumulate vs circulate): the proposal that *each day is a loop in which something from today can be converted into the imperishable and stored*, requiring that each resource be named and each conversion process be named — including **multistep** and **multicomponent** conversions. Validated via `/validate-idea` (keep-with-modification, 4 mods). Two-thirds of the proposal already existed and re-doing it would have violated DRY: the resources are named (personal-resource-inventory's six registers, with their own absorption rule) and the daily loop exists (neural-os-daily-loop). The load-bearing correction was **reframing from naming nodes to naming edges** — the conversion had never been a first-class object anywhere, appearing only as consequences scattered inside other pages' entries. Filed as a **candidate**: the promotion gate is N=3 named conversions and the count today is **1**.

| Components | Pattern | Unlock | Failure mode | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **The six registers** (personal-resource-inventory — the nodes, each with a distinct deploy mode) × **the converter ladder** (idle-resource-is-adversarial §The converter ladder — skill → tool → system, the one worked edge) × [CAST](./cast-overview.md) (nodes + edges) × [SPEAR](./spear-overview.md) (the edge is a procedure) × **the perishable/storable cut** (why the edge is urgent) × neural-os-daily-loop (the grain at which a perishable input expires) | **Adapter** (cross-class hand-off) + **Composite** (multistep: a chain of conversions is a conversion) + **Builder** (multicomponent: several non-substitutable inputs assemble one output) — three already-registered patterns, no new vocabulary | **The wiki had resource *nodes* and exactly one named *edge*; naming the edge-form generalizes it.** The converter ladder was filed as one specific route (hours → skill → tool → system) buried inside the Time-coupling glossary entry, yet it is an instance of a general move: **a conversion consumes one or more resource inputs and yields an output in a different class** — the point being the change of class, not an increase in amount. Generalizing supplies two shapes the ladder cannot express: **multistep** (a path — Composite) and **multicomponent** (a join — Builder), whose sharpest case is *hours + attention + a closing developmental window → a child's installed capability*, where the inputs do not substitute (you cannot spend more hours to reopen a closed window). New capability: a conversion is expressible as a CAST graph whose nodes are resource classes and whose edges are SPEAR procedures, so the layer needs **no new encoder and no acronym**. Daily instance: neural-os-daily-loop §The conversion slot, METER event `resource.conversion_logged`, pass-floor "one named conversion per day or the day's perishable budget is counted lost." | **Cataloguing all thirty ordered pairs** — six registers admit thirty edges, and enumerating them reproduces on the edges the sprawl the ledger's absorption rule prevents on the nodes; the bound is *a conversion earns a named entry only when its process is not derivable from its endpoints* (the ladder qualifies, "money → possession" does not — that is buying). **Re-naming the resources** — the six registers are owned upstream and the dependency must run one way, or promoting a seventh register breaks the catalogue. **Treating the daily slot as a journal** — it names a process, not a feeling, and zero is a legal answer; the tracked figure is the weekly rate. **Importing the doctrinal output class onto the systems page** — the same SRP guard the 2026-07-25 entry above already sets. | idle-resource-is-adversarial §Conversions (owner), personal-resource-inventory, neural-os-daily-loop, [software-design-principles-for-neural-os](./software-design-principles-for-neural-os.md), bible-money-stewardship-principles |

### 2026-08-17 Wireshark promotion ingest — 1 candidate unlock at the packet-analysis substrate

**Context.** Promoting `wireshark` from raw to wiki surfaced that the wiki already owned the *write* side of packet work (scapy-packet-crafting — forge a packet, `/`-stack the layers, `send()`) but had no *read* side. Wireshark supplies it: capture off the wire → filter → dissect. The two pages now bracket a single substrate from opposite directions.

| Components | Pattern | Unlock | Failure mode | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| scapy-packet-crafting (write: `Ether()/IP()/TCP()` → `send()`) × wireshark (read: capture → display filter → dissect) × the shared osi-glyph-grammar tower × the shared TCP-flags byte (Scapy's `TCP(flags=…)` == Wireshark's `tcp.flags.*`) | **read/write pair on one substrate** (opposite-direction sibling of a single OSI substrate) | **Packet-technique round-trip.** An operator who has drilled both sides can internalize a protocol by *forging a packet with Scapy and immediately proving it landed by reading Wireshark's dissection* — the fastest path to owning a protocol's byte layout, because the same byte is written and read within one loop. Distinct from either page alone: neither the write nor the read page, by itself, closes the feedback loop that makes byte-layout stick. | scapy-packet-crafting · wireshark |

**Falsifiable gate (why candidate, not confirmed).** The claim is that the *round-trip drill* teaches byte layout faster than drilling either side alone. Promote to confirmed only if a `wireshark.filter.drill` + `scapy.crafting.drill` paired session shows faster byte-layout recall (Erickson §0x400 checksum) than either drill run solo. If the paired drill shows no advantage over the write-side drill alone, the pair is a convenience cross-link, not an unlock — cut it to a plain related-pages link. (Note: the **offense↔defense catalog mirror** that Wireshark also instances is *not* registered here as new — it is a concrete Detective-tool instance of the already-confirmed 2026-06-05 M11 mirror unlock above; see wireshark §Defender's mirror.)

### 2026-08-31 Leahy *Worry Cure* ingest — 4 confirmed unlocks at the mental-wellbeing/self-regulation layer

**Context.** Five new pages (leahy-worry-cure, productive-vs-unproductive-worry, intolerance-of-uncertainty, worry-as-emotional-avoidance, health-anxiety) landed against four existing owner pages, and in every case the composition is already **built and reciprocally wired** — a routing rule, a precondition gate, or a named failure mode now lives on both sides, not just the new page. That reciprocal wiring is why these four register as confirmed rather than candidate.

| Components | Pattern | Unlock | Failure mode | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| productive-vs-unproductive-worry (3-gate triage: plausible · actionable-now · converts-to-a-step) × [thought-mastery-os](./thought-mastery-os.md) (single-thought distortion catch-and-counter cycle) | **Strategy — routing boundary between two intervention layers**, selected by loop-shape rather than content | **A chain of worry stops being treatable as a chain of thoughts.** Distortion-catching optimizes the *quality* of one stated thought; it has no natural stopping point against a cascade where every caught link is answered by the next what-if. The triage page supplies the missing dispatch rule: a discrete, single-object worry goes to the catch; an open-ended chain goes to a clock (worry time), which bounds the loop by construction instead of by resolution. | **Distortion-catch on an unbounded loop** — three or more distortions caught in one sitting with no movement on the PULSE S-axis; the catching becomes the worrying and feels like progress while doing none. Named on both owner pages. | productive-vs-unproductive-worry, [thought-mastery-os](./thought-mastery-os.md) |
| productive-vs-unproductive-worry triage gate × resilience-and-recovery-os ambient-anxiety collapse type (diagnose-the-collapse → pick-a-move) | **Precondition gate inserted before a move-menu selection** — a Stage 1 (triage) that must clear before Stage 2 (recovery move) fires | **Ambient-anxiety recovery stops being a guess between six moves.** The taxonomy previously named "externalize the loop + bounded action" as the right shape for ambient anxiety without a procedure; the triage page supplies it as two ordered stages — classify the worry, *then* pick the move — so a plausible, actionable worry routes straight to [problem-solving-os](./problem-solving-os.md) instead of consuming a recovery move it does not need. | **Triage-skipping** — running any recovery move on a *productive* worry; the loop returns unchanged the moment the move ends because the real (actionable) problem was never addressed. Named on resilience-and-recovery-os. | productive-vs-unproductive-worry, resilience-and-recovery-os |
| intolerance-of-uncertainty (affective — can I sit with not knowing?) × [decision-kernel](./decision-kernel.md) Q6 + reversibility rule (epistemic — what's cheap to learn before committing?) | **Axis split on a shared word** — two owners on either side of one distinguishing question | **One observable question sorts which loop is running: has the search stopped changing the answer?** If yes, the epistemic work is done and the residual unknown is an affective-tolerance problem, not more decision work — gathering further evidence at that point is a safety behavior wearing the Decision Kernel's clothes, and running another Kernel pass makes it worse by dignifying the search as analysis. | **Info-gathering on a closed decision** (searching after the answer stopped moving) and its mirror, **treating a genuine information gap as an intolerance problem** (declaring acceptance instead of doing five minutes of cheap research). Both named on intolerance-of-uncertainty; the routing question is now on [decision-kernel](./decision-kernel.md) too. | intolerance-of-uncertainty, [decision-kernel](./decision-kernel.md) |
| worry-as-emotional-avoidance (verbal-loop suppresses the arousal a body-scan is built to detect) × developing-eq Layer 1 body-scan drill | **Precondition gate inserted before a measurement is trusted** — a channel check ("am I verbal-looping right now?") gating whether a clean scan counts as a pass | **EQ-drill compliance stops being confused with EQ capability.** An operator mid-worry-loop passes the body-scan and naming drills honestly — no sensation to find, nothing to name — while the emotion is present and undetected; the two scores were pointing in opposite directions and nothing on developing-eq could tell them apart before this gate existed. The fix is a precondition, not a rewrite: run the channel check first, and a clean scan taken mid-loop is a null result, not a pass. | The **articulate-calm false negative** — self-report reads calm and rational, the body scan returns "nothing here" and gets scored as a pass, and only the delayed post-episode tension/irritability rebound reveals the emotion was there all along. Named on both worry-as-emotional-avoidance and developing-eq. | worry-as-emotional-avoidance, developing-eq |

## Candidate unlocks (proposed, not yet built/tested)

These are compositions surfaced during analysis that are plausible but haven't been built into a worked artifact. Highest-leverage experimental directions.

| Components                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Hypothesised unlock                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Status                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| [structure-first](./structure-first.md) (encode structure before content) + its four mode-methods: [zoom-in-zoom-out](./zoom-in-zoom-out.md) (text) · [CAST](./cast-overview.md) top-graph-first (systems) · [SCREAM](./semantic-listening-system.md) (live speech) · [ORIENT](./orient-method.md) layout pass (space) | **Modality-routed structure-first (a Facade)** — the four were stated in the raw book as separate techniques; naming their shared parent turns them into one principle with a *routing key = input modality*. Recognizing whether new material is text / graph / speech / space selects the correct first-move in one step, without re-deriving the principle each time — and a modality with no method shows up as an empty routing cell (a completeness checksum over the four). New capability neither the principle nor any single method has alone: modality → method becomes a lookup. Failure mode: applying the wrong mode's method — trying to zoom-out/zoom-in a **live conversation you cannot re-read**, when speech needs SCREAM's buckets-first *precisely because* there is no scrollback; second failure, "reading" a handed-over structure instead of building it, which loses the contrast that makes any of the four work ([bedrock](./bedrock.md) §The survival filter). Promotes from candidate on **1 worked instance** where naming the modality first measurably changed the method chosen on genuinely new material; demote to a paragraph on [structure-first](./structure-first.md) if no such instance in ~3 months. *(2026-08-27, Zoom ingest.)* | candidate |
| [lego-skills-patterns](./lego-skills-patterns.md) spiral/leash + [edge-sign](./edge-sign.md) loop-sign check + [delay-encoding-in-cast](./delay-encoding-in-cast.md) gap + system-archetypes-overview roster | **Archetype recognition without archetype templates** — the eleven archetypes decompose without remainder into loops, signs and gaps that are already encoded for their own reasons, so what distinguishes one from another is only *how the loops attach*: five couplings (shared node · shared stock · mirror pair · drifting anchor · lone loop + gap) cover the roster. Eleven installs become five, and the archetype is *derived* rather than stored — the same generator-over-trace move [dynamic-edge-encoding](./dynamic-edge-encoding.md) makes for behaviour. Owner archetype-encoding-in-cast. Failure mode: naming a coupling on loops whose signs were never multiplied — a misread spiral/leash makes every downstream call confidently wrong, which is why the loop-sign check is a hard step and not a nicety. | candidate |
| [delay-encoding-in-cast](./delay-encoding-in-cast.md) gap (candidate) + [encoding-quantities-in-cast](./encoding-quantities-in-cast.md) thickness (candidate) + [dynamic-edge-encoding](./dynamic-edge-encoding.md) profile (candidate) | **A behaviour-over-time graph drawn inside the edge scene** — the gap is a time axis (near end = departing now, far end = arriving, having left one delay ago), so varying the thickness *along* it plots the rate's recent history at zero extra cost: uniform is steady, beaded is bursty, thickening is rising. Neither modifier alone reaches it — thickness without a gap has no axis to vary along, a gap without thickness has nothing to plot. It also separates the two readings of a full pipeline: rate × delay is the long-run average (Little's Law), the profile is *why it is full right now*. Failure mode: drawing a profile for behaviour a loop already generates — a trace where the graph holds the generator, which the owner page's own METER floor is built to catch. | candidate |
| [delay-encoding-in-cast](./delay-encoding-in-cast.md) scene gap (candidate) + [encoding-quantities-in-cast](./encoding-quantities-in-cast.md) edge rate (candidate) | **In-flight visibility** — the material an edge is holding between cause and effect equals rate × delay (Little's Law, `L = λW`), and the scene's gap is where it visibly sits. A torrent across a long gap is a large hidden buffer of stale work that *neither endpoint holds* — invisible in an undelayed encoding, readable on sight once both modifiers ride the edge. It also repairs the flow-balance checksum, which assumes instant transfer: across a delayed edge, inflow is what departed one delay ago, and that offset is the overshoot mechanism. Failure mode: a duration peg without its unit — the arithmetic runs and means nothing. | candidate |
| [delay-encoding-in-cast](./delay-encoding-in-cast.md) scene gap (candidate) + [lego-skills-patterns](./lego-skills-patterns.md) spiral/leash + system-delays-and-lags §Time Constants | **Oscillation read off the picture** — a balancing loop's behaviour stops being asserted and becomes derivable: the pattern gives the loop type, the gap gives the lag, and the delay-to-time-constant ratio decides whether it settles or overshoots. A leash carrying a long gap is a controller that will oscillate, and that is a graph-level *prediction made by looking* — the capability CAST is for and the one an undelayed encoding cannot reach. Sister to the loop-sign checksum, which derives loop *polarity* the same way this derives loop *stability*. Failure mode: calling oscillation from the gap alone without the time constant — a long delay in a slow system is fine; it is the ratio that bites. | candidate |
| [encoding-quantities-in-cast](./encoding-quantities-in-cast.md) Q0–Q2 edge rates (candidate) + node levels (CAST and Georgian Node System.md §Major system and SEM3) + stocks-and-flows-foundations `Flow = d(Stock)/dt` | **Flow-balance checksum** — at any node, Σ(in-edge rates) − Σ(out-edge rates) must equal the rate of change of that node's encoded level; a mismatch localizes a missing edge or a Q2 vessel misread as a rate, before the graph is trusted. The capability neither half has alone: CAST verifies *structure* by counting nodes and has no check on magnitudes, while the stock/flow identity is arithmetic with no encoding to run against. **The checksum is also a generator** — the three quantities are one equation, so encoding any two makes the third derivable and quantity encoding can *reduce* what is stored (the spanning-tree move from compression-for-comprehension-framework applied to magnitudes). Failure mode: walking the balance on rates that were never comparable — mixing per-second and per-day edges, so the arithmetic is clean and the reading is meaningless; Q1 pegs must carry their unit or the check validates garbage. *(2026-08-31: stub filled; magnitude as a Stream property, not a fifth slot.)* | candidate |
| [edge-sign](./edge-sign.md) (+/− per-edge polarity, candidate) + [lego-skills-patterns](./lego-skills-patterns.md) spiral/leash + [nodes-and-edges](./nodes-and-edges.md) loop holds | **Loop-sign checksum** — walk any cycle and multiply the edge signs: an even count of − must land on the spiral (amplifying), an odd count on the leash (stabilizing); a mismatch means a mis-signed edge or a mis-classified loop, caught before the graph is trusted. Loop polarity becomes *derivable from the encoding* instead of asserted at pattern-pick time — an integrity capability neither the pattern library nor the edge scenes have alone. Failure mode: signs assigned from node vibes ("this node is negative") instead of a per-edge causal reading — the product check then validates garbage. *(2026-07-09 `/validate-idea`: keep-with-modification — polarity as additive modifier, not axis swap.)* | candidate |
| [word-knowledge-links](./word-knowledge-links.md) (six directed links between the three word-stores) × [encoded-spaced-repetition](./encoded-spaced-repetition.md) (Recognition · Recall · Discrimination · Diagnosis) × [tip-of-the-tongue](./tip-of-the-tongue.md) (the store model both stand on) | **Two-coordinate card audit** — the two decompositions are *orthogonal*, not rivals: ESR names **which retrieval operation** a card drills, the link lens names **between which two stores**, and every card carries both coordinates (the French dictation card is `phonological→visual × Recall`). Neither axis alone can say what a deck actually trains — a deck can be complete on one and empty on the other, which is exactly how a 697-word deck with 353 hand-authored mnemonics scored 3/6 with the entire visual store unpaved. New capability: a vocabulary deck becomes *auditable before it is built*, and a gap gets named as a missing link rather than diagnosed as "these words didn't stick". Load-bearing scoring rule: **a revealed answer is not a trained link** — unless the output is produced and checked, score it untrained. Failure mode: collapsing the two axes into one list (six links + four operations read as ten card types), which double-counts and hides that they multiply rather than add; second failure, chasing 6/6 as a target when coverage is a design decision (a reading-only deck is legitimately 2/6). Promotes from candidate only when a **second, independent** deck audit surfaces an untrained link its owner had not already named; zero further instances in ~3 months demotes it to a paragraph on [vocabulary-word-type-routing](./vocabulary-word-type-routing.md). *(2026-07-20 `/validate-idea`: keep-with-modification — lens not framework, no acronym, stores stay owned by [tip-of-the-tongue](./tip-of-the-tongue.md). Falsifier satisfied once: `tools/french-1000`.)* | candidate |
| [bedrock](./bedrock.md) (Bahrick permastore / Neisser critical threshold, generalized) × [lifecycle-manager](./lifecycle-manager.md) (Active → Cold → Archive → Drop) × [spaced-repetition](./spaced-repetition.md) (Bjork storage-vs-retrieval strength) | **Retirement by success** — the lifecycle layer's four triggers all retire *failures* (leeches, disposables, superseded cards, dead contexts); none can say "this card is retired because it was learned too well to need reviewing again." Bedrock supplies that missing **positive** exit, and the composition gives spaced repetition something it did not have on its own: **a finish line**. The review cost becomes an investment that terminates rather than a permanent subscription, which is the actual argument for paying it. The exit is deliberately *parallel* to the retirement ladder rather than a fifth rung — a Cold card stopped being worth its cost, a Graduated card settled its debt, and collapsing them destroys exactly the signal worth tracking. Second-order capability: the graduated pool is a **standing falsifier**, since a quarterly 5% spot-check turns "I know this permanently" from an article of faith into a measured revocation rate. Failure mode: declaring graduation on felt confidence, which is [fluency-illusion](./fluency-illusion.md) wearing a permanence mask — the gate is behavioural (involuntary-fire test + zero reviews across a ≥3-month interval + fast correct answer), never introspective; second failure, deleting graduated cards, which makes the spot-check impossible and the claim unfalsifiable. Promotes from candidate when the **first cohort clears a ≥3-month cold probe** with a revocation rate at or under 1-in-10; a higher rate falsifies the criterion rather than the concept, and tightens the gate. *(2026-07-30 `/validate-idea`: keep-with-modification — the modification was requiring a falsifiable declaration criterion, shipped as [bedrock](./bedrock.md)'s involuntary-fire test plus the cold probe.)* | candidate |
| [substitute-word-system](./substitute-word-system.md) §Onset priority (Advance's *encode-the-onset* rule) + [Major System](./mnemonic-methods-master.md) precompiled-lookup architecture + [multilingual peg substrate](./polyglot-architecture.md) | **Onset Peg Alphabet** ([onset-peg-alphabet](./onset-peg-alphabet.md)) — freeze one fixed peg image per recurring word-onset into a reusable table, drilled to reflex, so encoding a new word is onset-lookup + rime-peg instead of fresh full-word decomposition. Converts the slow System-2 decomposition into a System-1 lookup (the deciding [CAST](./cast-overview.md) live-encode-speed criterion) and caches the cross-language peg search once per onset. Load-bearing collision rule: the frozen onset is a *surname*, the rime carries identity, so `swim`/`swarm`/`swing` safely reuse one pig. Failure mode: pegging only the onset and leaving the rime vague → every shared-onset word collapses into one blur (mass interference). Failure mode: over-fitting to the 2014 bank's phrasing. | candidate |

Beats [PAO](./person-action-object-system.md)'s 6 digits/locus with ~6 newly installed items instead of ~300, and adds the two capabilities no traditional system has: the stored form loads back into the soroban (storage format = compute format), and recall self-verifies (seal mismatch = known error before use). Failure modes: flame/rose +5-overlay confusion across slots; tier-skipping into L3 before matrix-cell fluency; over-sealing trivial numbers (ceremony); cell interference with [calendar-memory](./calendar-memory.md) if journeys are shared. *(2026-07-02 `/validate-idea`: keep-with-modification; candidate until the 24h falsifier vs PAO+Major is passed.)* | candidate |

Fixes the weak link of the mental-soroban transition: bead states are systematic but not distinctive (`7` vs `8` = one bead), while cube states are vivid *and* keep moves cheap (friend-of-5 = rose toggle + face step). Hall's Marriage Theorem formalizes this: a perfect matching between a need-set and a candidate-set exists iff every subset S of needs has at least |S| candidates who can satisfy at least one need in S. Operational consequence: a "no" from the matching gate isn't just a vague preference — it's the formal failure of Hall's condition on the visible evidence. CAST upgrade: given a palace of n loci, the minimum link count for reliable traversal is ≈ (n log n)/2 — a concrete architectural constraint, not a vague "link generously" heuristic. Getting wealthy: boldness, risk, scale. Staying wealthy: the Dichotomy discipline (balance producer urgency with stewardship calm), the Daily discipline (consistency over heroics), the Demand discipline (market-pull, not passion-push). New capability: getting-wealthy-vs-staying-wealthy now has an operational checklist, not just a behavioural warning. Neither alone is sufficient: psychology without structure produces good intentions that crumble under cashflow pressure; structure without psychology produces rigid rules that get rationalised away at first difficulty. ## How to add an entry

When a new composition surfaces:

1. **Identify all components** by their owner-page wiki-links. Never list a component that doesn't have a page; instead, write the page first.
2. **Name the architectural pattern.** If it's a new pattern (not [substrate-algorithm-composition](./substrate-algorithm-composition.md)), add it to that page's sister-pattern list before referencing here.
3. **State the unlock as a capability sentence**: "X, without Y" or "Z to a degree neither A nor B can reach alone." The bar is *qualitatively new*, not just "faster."
4. **Name the failure mode**: the specific thing that breaks if the composition is botched. If you can't name one, the composition probably isn't real.
5. **Status**: start at `candidate`; promote to `confirmed` only when a worked artifact exists and the mechanism is understood.

---

## Patterns currently registered

**Five confirmed architectural primitives** as of 2026-05-25:

- [substrate-algorithm-composition](./substrate-algorithm-composition.md) — algorithm × state-substrate = capability bounded by neither. ~15 confirmed instances.
- glyph-grammar-pattern — finite shape alphabet + composition rules + hybrid slot architecture + ambient/local fallback rule. 3 confirmed instances (code-glyph-grammar, aws-glyph-grammar, math-proof-glyph-grammar).
- [recognition-gym-pattern](./recognition-gym-pattern.md) — Red Queen Gym instance drilling classification under tight timer through Lamp/Scale/Sword phases. 3 confirmed instances (construct, crux, fallacy).
- [internal-limits-pattern](./internal-limits-pattern.md) — **PROMOTED 2026-05-25 (Wave 4)** — sufficiently expressive systems have internal limits seen from inside, not external walls beyond. **6 confirmed cross-domain instances** as of Wave 7 (language: TLP 5.6; mathematics: Gödel; computation: Turing's halting problem; physics: light-speed limit; phenomenology: Husserl-Heidegger horizon; **AI safety verification: Rice's theorem ([internal-limits-applied-to-ai-safety](./internal-limits-applied-to-ai-safety.md), Wave 7**)). 2 candidate instances queued. **Highest cross-domain spread of any registered primitive.**
- [cross-tradition-convergence-pattern](./cross-tradition-convergence-pattern.md) — **PROMOTED 2026-05-25 (Wave 7)** from Wave 5 candidate-pattern. When ≥3 traditions with no causal lineage independently articulate the same structural epistemic distinction, the distinction is META-substantively real. **5+ confirmed instances** of show-vs-say-like convergence: Hebrews 11:1 · TLP · Plato · Hindu Vedanta · Zen · apophatic Christian theology. **Highest *evidential* strength of any wiki META-pattern** because the convergence cannot be explained by causal lineage.

When a sixth pattern accrues enough instances to deserve its own owner page, promote it. Track candidates in the *Pattern* column of the tables above; if a single pattern appears in 3+ rows without an owner page, that's the trigger to factor it out.

**Candidate patterns (not yet promoted)**:

- **Recognition gym** — **PROMOTED TO OWNER PAGE [recognition-gym-pattern](./recognition-gym-pattern.md) on 2026-05-25** by the [Fallacy-Recognition Gym](./fallacy-taxonomy.md) joining as the 3rd instance. A [Red Queen Gym](./red-queen-skill-gym.md) instance that drills *recognition-of-which-pattern-applies* under a tight time-budget, with telemetry on per-pattern accuracy + recognition latency + false-positive rate. Build recipe (per the owner page): Corpus (calibrated, labeled, difficulty-graded, ≥100 items) + Timer (per-item; tight; matched to item complexity) + Phases (Lamp = recognition; Scale = discrimination; Sword = cold pressure). Three confirmed instances: construct-recognition-gym (12 code constructs, 6-s window), [crux-recognition-gym](./crux-recognition-gym.md) (17 puzzle archetypes, 60-s window), [Fallacy-Recognition Gym](./fallacy-taxonomy.md) (~25 fallacies + 2 formal, 60-s window). The 10× window difference reflects narrative-noise overhead. Uniform METER schema: `recognition_accuracy` · `recognition_latency_p50/p95` · `phase` · `false_positive_rate` — allows cross-instance aggregation. **Status: confirmed pattern, owner page exists. Move to §Patterns currently registered list on next pass.**

- **Reserved-icon vocabulary** — a small finite set of distinctive images is reserved for *structural operators* in some domain, removing those images from the domain's primary encoding pool but making structure recoverable. Currently 2 instances: code-memorization visual grammar (room=function, door=call, fork=if, loop=circle, …) for code structure, and [major-system-for-mathematical-notation](./major-system-for-mathematical-notation.md) operator icons (`·` decimal, `−` minus, exponent arrows, repeating-marker, `≈`) for numeric structure. The trade-off (every reserved icon costs one cell from the digit/scene pool, discipline rule ≤6) is identical across both instances. One more instance triggers promotion to its own owner page.

- **Builder ↔ recognizer pair** — a slow, designed protocol that *constructs* an item (System 2) paired with a fast timed gym that *recognizes* it (System 1), sharing one slot as the handoff. Currently 1 instance: [prism-pattern-discovery](./prism-pattern-discovery.md) × [recognition-gym-pattern](./recognition-gym-pattern.md) (the Pattern Card's *Recognition cues* = the gym stimulus). Sibling shape to production-reception-grammar-pair. Promote at N=3 — or, per PRISM's own ladder, when the pair predicts its next instance before it is found (level 5). Registered 2026-08-27.

- **Bottleneck relocation** — *"a capacity move spends an abundant resource to buy a scarce axis; the binding bottleneck is relocated, never removed."* Registered from [PRISM](./prism-pattern-discovery.md) run #3 as the rule `bottleneck-relocation-trade` at **ladder level 5** (2026-08-27-prism-runs). It earns a row here — and the other two PRISM rules deliberately do not — because it is the only one of the three that crosses a domain boundary: engineering (system-design-fundamentals — cache · read replica · shard · queue, each buying latency or throughput with consistency, complexity or freshness) and *cognition* (tools-over-intelligence §"The bottleneck doesn't vanish — it relocates", where instrumenting a task moves the constraint from raw capacity to tool-building). Amdahl and Goldratt supply the targeting clause the wiki had implicitly: a trade pays only when it is aimed at the *currently binding* constraint. Counterexample on file: caching a write-bound system — spends complexity, buys nothing. **Promotion needs a third domain that is neither engineering nor cognition**, or a level-6 first-hand construction derived from the mechanism; per the ladder, *not* a third row. The register of standing levels for all PRISM rules lives at [prism-pattern-discovery](./prism-pattern-discovery.md) §Rule register, not here — single-domain rules would bury the cross-domain signal this list exists to hold. Registered 2026-08-28.

- **Surface-decoy / deep-invariant** — *"surface features diverge, one deep mechanism unifies, surface-only holders false-recognize."* Surfaced by three live [PRISM](./prism-pattern-discovery.md) runs 2026-08-27 (2026-08-27-prism-runs): cybersec (BSWR code-data-confusion), algorithms (CLRS invariant), system design (bottleneck-relocation trade). **PROMOTION REFUSED at the structural 3rd instance** — the worked instance of PRISM ladder unlock ②. Two reasons the row-count is misleading: (a) *method-artifact* — PRISM's Inspect/Agreement step is built to find deep structure under surface diversity, so the instances are not independent (the tautological part sits at ladder **level 2**); (b) *pre-stated decoy* — run #3's deep principle was stated up front by system-design-fundamentals, so nothing was hidden. The **non-tautological core** worth keeping: surface-sorting ⇒ novice error, deep-sorting ⇒ expert transfer ([novice-vs-expert-cognition](./novice-vs-expert-cognition.md)) — *not* forced by the method (ladder **level 3**). **Status: reframed to that core; HELD, not promoted** — needs a run where the mechanism is not pre-stated and the method-artifact is controlled. **Structural 4th instance 2026-08-28** (2026-08-28-prism-run4-french-phonology, French phonology: silent letters · nasals · liaison · agreement `-e` over one coda-position mechanism) — **refusal stands**, because the method-artifact is still uncontrolled. One weak new datum *against* pure artifact: that corpus's author independently found the shallow layer ("read one letter ahead") and stopped there, so the surface/deep gap is not solely produced by running PRISM. Not enough to promote; a controlled instance is still the missing evidence. This entry is itself the demonstration that PRISM's ladder corrects the mechanical "3+ rows → owner page" rule. Registered 2026-08-27, refused 2026-08-27.

- **~~Glyph-grammar pattern~~** ✅ **Promoted to owner page**: glyph-grammar-pattern (2026-05-13). ✅ **First instance runnable end-to-end** (2026-05-20). The pattern is *finite shape alphabet + composition rules + hybrid slot architecture (frame atmosphere / per-piece ornaments / header banner / dominance pattern) + ambient/local fallback rule* = at-a-glance encoding of a specific instance of a structured-system domain as a single non-destructive image. The silhouette names the structural pattern; the styling names the specific contextual variant; both are read in one fixation. **Three registered instances** (as of 2026-05-13): code-glyph-grammar (code structure × programming-language identity, 11 pieces, 12 concept-spaces from programming-language-concept-spaces; left-to-right = sequence), aws-glyph-grammar (AWS architecture × deployment-mode identity, 11 pieces, 12 architectural-decision axes; left-to-right = data flow), and [math-proof-glyph-grammar](./math-proof-glyph-grammar.md) (proof structure × formality/domain identity, 11 pieces, 12 mathematical-decision axes; top-to-bottom = logical dependency). The third instance validated the pattern's domain-portability beyond system architecture and surfaced two refinements that propagated back to the owner page: (a) reading direction is domain-dependent, not universally left-to-right; (b) container-piece set is open per-instance (▢ in all three; ◆ added by AWS; ⟲ and ⊥ added by math-proof). Promotion-before-third-instance was justified by the AWS pressure-test; the third instance confirmed promotion was the right call. **2026-05-20 — code-glyph-grammar promoted from prose to a runnable language** via programming-mental-language-roadmap: 11 SVG primitives + composition-rule schema + decoration schema + AST → SVG pipeline for Python (stdlib `ast`) and Java/Rust (tree-sitter) + 6-algorithm × 3-language demo battery + 3-pane interactive viewer (source · silhouette · REMAPS scene). The alphabet held empirically: same 11 pieces sufficed for 3 stress tests (async crawler, query-builder class, streaming JSON parser) and for 6 canonical algorithms parsed from native Java + Rust source. The hourglass-overload (await + yield) question resolved by corpus data (95.82% / 1.86% / 2.10% / 0.22% across the Python 3.14 stdlib, 67K functions): keep the compression. Artifacts in `wiki/assets/glyphs/` and `tools/glyph-composer/`. Candidate fourth+ instances: network topology, business process / BPMN, system-design interview diagrams, ML pipeline architecture, distributed-systems consensus protocols, organizational structure, biological pathway, narrative/storytelling arc, liturgical structure.
